Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the approximation object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 969. The moduli space has dimension at most 230 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 969 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the approximation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 230. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on approximation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 867. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 206 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on approximation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 867. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 206. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)
